% Lange Formel-/Begründungspaare erhalten innerhalb dieses Bandes automatisch
% eine echte Fortsetzungszeile, damit die Beweistabellen im Satzspiegel bleiben.
\let\proofstepwidestar\proofstepwidestarbreak
\providecommand{\ProofRefStack}[1]{%
  \ensuremath{\begin{gathered}#1\end{gathered}}}

\chapter{Einführung}

Dieser Band stellt die kombinatorischen Werkzeuge bereit, mit denen später
Produkte beliebiger endlicher Länge behandelt werden. Endliche Wörter werden
als spezielle endliche Folgen konstruiert. Volle planare Binärbäume werden
anschließend durch eindeutig zerlegbare Wörter codiert. Insbesondere werden
weder eine Wortstruktur noch eine Baumstruktur axiomatisch vorausgesetzt.
Induktion und Rekursion auf diesen Mengen sind Sätze, die auf der natürlichen
Induktion und dem Dedekindschen Rekursionssatz beruhen.

Die Trennung ist für die folgenden Bände wesentlich: Erst Band~23 führt mit
der Halbgruppe wieder eine axiomatisch definierte algebraische Struktur ein.
Assoziativität wird dort genau einmal im Blockgesetz benutzt; die übrige
Umklammerungsaussage folgt aus den hier konstruierten Werkzeugen.

\chapter{Endliche Wörter}

\section{Wortmenge und Länge}

Ein Wort über \(A\) ist der Funktionsgraph einer nullbasierten endlichen
Folge mit Werten in \(A\). Die folgende Definition ist durch
\(\powerset(\mathbb N\times A)\) gebunden und bezeichnet daher eine Menge.

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Menge der endlichen Wörter]{%
  \FiniteWords{A}\coloneqq
  \bigl\{w\in\powerset(\mathbb N\times A)\bigm|
    \exists n\in\mathbb N\,
      (w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A)\bigr\}%
}{FiniteWordSetDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep n\dsep\FiniteWords{A}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Endliche Wörter}{\FiniteWords{A}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierte endliche Folgen sind endliche Wörter]{%
  n\in\mathbb N\dsep w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A
  \vdash w\in\FiniteWords{A}%
}{FiniteWordFromTyping}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep i\dsep n\dsep w\dsep z}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{n\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{w\subseteq\NatSeg{n}\times A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{Funktion}[def]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\NatSeg{n}\subseteq\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSubsetN}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{w\subseteq\mathbb N\times A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm],
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\times B\eqvdash
      \exists a\in A\,\exists b\in B\,x=(a,b)}[thm],
    \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash
      a\in A\land b\in B}[thm]{3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{w\in\powerset(\mathbb N\times A)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordSetDef}[def]{6,1,2}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Eindeutige Länge eines endlichen Wortes]{%
  w\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
  \exists!n\in\mathbb N\,
    (w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A)%
}{FiniteWordLengthUnique}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep m\dsep n}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \exists n\in\mathbb N\,(w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordSetDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{m\in\mathbb N\land
    w\colon\NatSeg{m}\to A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[4]{n\in\mathbb N\land
    w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{\NatSeg{m}=\NatSeg{n}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{Funktion}[def]{3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{\NatSeg m\subseteq\NatSeg n}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A=B\vdash A\subseteq B}[thm]{5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{\NatSeg n\subseteq\NatSeg m}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A=B\vdash B\subseteq A}[thm]{5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{m\leq n}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegLeqSubset}[thm]{3,4,6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{n\leq m}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegLeqSubset}[thm]{3,4,7}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{m=n}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoLeqAntisymmetric}[thm]{3,4,8,9}}
  \proofstepwidestar{%
    \forall m\,\forall n\,\Bigl(
      \bigl(m\in\mathbb N\land w\colon\NatSeg{m}\to A\bigr)
      \land
      \bigl(n\in\mathbb N\land w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A\bigr)
      \rightarrow m=n\Bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rUI{\rRI{\rAI{3,4},10}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar{%
    \existsleone n\,\bigl(n\in\mathbb N\land
      w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\existsleone x\,P(x)\eqvdash
      \forall y\forall z((P(y)\land P(z))\rightarrow y=z)}[thm]{11}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \exists!n\in\mathbb N\,(w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\exists x\,P(x),\; \existsleone x\,P(x)
      \vdash \exists! x\,P(x)}[thm]{2,12}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Länge eines endlichen Wortes]{%
  w\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
  \WordLength{w}\coloneqq
  \iota n\in\mathbb N\,
    (w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A)%
}{FiniteWordLengthDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep n}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Wortlänge}{\WordLength{w}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung durch die Wortlänge]{%
  w\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
  \WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N\dsep
  w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A%
}{FiniteWordTyping}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \exists!n\,\bigl(n\in\mathbb N\land
      w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthUnique}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N\land
      w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{2},
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N}{\rAEa{3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{\rAEb{3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Länge aus einer gegebenen Worttypisierung]{%
  w\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep n\in\mathbb N\dsep
  w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A
  \vdash \WordLength{w}=n%
}{FiniteWordLengthFromTyping}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep n\dsep k}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{n\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \exists!k\in\mathbb N\,
      \bigl(w\colon\NatSeg{k}\to A\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthUnique}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N\land
    w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
    \rAI{4,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[2,3]{%
    n\in\mathbb N\land w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A}{%
    \rAI{2,3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{\WordLength{w}=n}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\exists! x\,P(x),\; P(a),\; P(b)
      \vdash a=b}[thm]{6,7,8}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\section{Leerwort, Buchstabenwörter und Nichtleerwörter}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Leerwort]{%
  \EmptyWord{A}\coloneqq\varnothing%
}{EmptyWordDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep\EmptyWord{A}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Leerwort}{\EmptyWord{A}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Das Leerwort hat Länge null]{%
  \EmptyWord{A}\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
  \WordLength{\EmptyWord{A}}=0%
}{EmptyWordLength}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar{0\in\mathbb N}{\FormulaRefAuto{PeanoZero}[ax]}
  \proofstepwidestar{\NatSeg{0}=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegZero}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar{\EmptyWord{A}=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{EmptyWordDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar{\varnothing\colon\varnothing\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\varnothing\colon\varnothing\to M}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar{\EmptyWord{A}\colon\NatSeg{0}\to A}{%
    \rIE{2,3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar{\EmptyWord{A}\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{1,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar{\WordLength{\EmptyWord{A}}=0}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthFromTyping}[thm]{6,1,5}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Menge der nichtleeren Wörter]{%
  \NonemptyWords{A}\coloneqq
  \FiniteWords{A}\setminus\{\EmptyWord{A}\}%
}{NonemptyWordSetDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep\FiniteWords{A}\dsep\NonemptyWords{A}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Nichtleere Wörter}{\NonemptyWords{A}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Elementkriterium für nichtleere Wörter]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\eqvdash
  w\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep\WordLength{w}\neq0%
}{NonemptyWordMembership}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwide{\(\vdash\)}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\in\FiniteWords{A}\land
      w\notin\{\EmptyWord{A}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordSetDef}[def]{1},
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\setminus B\eqvdash
        x\in A\land x\notin B}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rAEa{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\notin\{\EmptyWord{A}\}}{\rAEb{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\neq\EmptyWord{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{\WordLength{w}=0}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{w\colon\varnothing\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegZero}[thm]{6,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{w=\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{f\colon\varnothing\to M\vdash
        f=\varnothing}[thm]{8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{w=\EmptyWord{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{EmptyWordDef}[def]{9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{\bot}{\rBI{5,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\neq0}{\rCI{6,11}}
  \closeproofpartwide

  \proofpartwide{\(\dashv\)}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{\WordLength{w}\neq0}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{w=\EmptyWord{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,3]{\WordLength{w}=0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{EmptyWordLength}[thm]{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{\bot}{\rBI{2,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{w\neq\EmptyWord{A}}{\rCI{3,5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{w\notin\{\EmptyWord{A}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm]{6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordSetDef}[def],
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\setminus B\eqvdash
        x\in A\land x\notin B}[thm]{1,7}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung nichtleerer Wörter]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  w\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
  \WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N\dsep
  w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A%
}{NonemptyWordTyping}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{2}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Buchstabenwort]{%
  a\in A\vdash
  \LetterWord{a}\coloneqq\{(0,a)\}%
}{LetterWordDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep\LetterWord{a}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Buchstabenwort}{\LetterWord{a}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Grundgesetze der Buchstabenwörter]{%
  a\in A\vdash
  \LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}\dsep
  \WordLength{\LetterWord{a}}=1\dsep
  \LetterWord{a}(0)=a%
}{LetterWordFacts}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar{1\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoOneInN}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LetterWord{a}\subseteq\NatSeg{1}\times A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordDef}[def]{1},
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegOneMembership}[thm],
    \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash
      a\in A\land b\in B}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \forall i\in\NatSeg{1}\,\exists!b\in A\,
      \bigl((i,b)\in\LetterWord{a}\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordDef}[def]{1},
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegOneMembership}[thm],
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm],
    \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)=(c,d)\eqvdash
      a=c\land b=d}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\colon\NatSeg{1}\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{UniqueValuedGraphFunction}[thm]{3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{2,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{\LetterWord{a}}=1}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthFromTyping}[thm]{6,2,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar{1\neq0}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoOne}[def],
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccNonzero}[ax]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{6,7,8}}
  \proofstepwidestar{0\in\NatSeg{1}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegOneMembership}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    (0,\LetterWord{a}(0))\in\LetterWord{a}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
      (x,F(x))\in F}[thm]{5,10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}(0)=a}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordDef}[def]{11},
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm],
    \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)=(c,d)\eqvdash
      a=c\land b=d}[thm]}
\end{tabproofwide}

\section{Konkatenation}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Mitgliedschaft in verschobenen Bildmengen]{%
  x\in\{r+j\mid j\in J\}\eqvdash
  \exists j\,(j\in J\land x=r+j)%
}{TranslatedImageSetMembership}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{J\dsep j\dsep r\dsep x}
}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Konkatenation endlicher Wörter]{%
  u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
  \WordConcat{u}{v}\coloneqq
  u\cup
  \bigl\{(\WordLength{u}+j,v(j))\bigm|
    j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\bigr\}%
}{WordConcatenationDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v\dsep j}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Wortkonkatenation}{\WordConcat{u}{v}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Blockzerlegung eines Anfangsabschnitts]{%
  r,s\in\mathbb N\vdash
  \NatSeg{r+s}
  =\NatSeg{r}\cup
    \bigl\{r+j\bigm|j\in\NatSeg{s}\bigr\}%
}{WordIndexBlockDecomposition}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{j\dsep r\dsep s}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideind[Induktionsanfang]{%
    r\in\mathbb N\vdash
    \NatSeg{r+0}=\NatSeg r\cup
      \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg 0\}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{r\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar{\NatSeg 0=\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegZero}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall x\,\Bigl(x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg 0\}
      \leftrightarrow
      \exists j\,(j\in\NatSeg 0\land x=r+j)\Bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TranslatedImageSetMembership}[def]}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall x\,x\notin\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg 0\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x \not\in \varnothing}[thm]{2,3}}
    \proofstepwidestar{\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg 0\}=\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\forall x\,(x\in A\leftrightarrow x\in B)
        \vdash A=B}[ax]{4,
        \FormulaRefAuto{x \not\in \varnothing}[thm]}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{r+0=r}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddRightZero}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \NatSeg{r+0}=\NatSeg r\cup
        \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg 0\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\cup\varnothing=A}[thm]{5,6}}
  \closeproofpartwideind

  \proofpartwideind[Induktionsschritt]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &r,s\in\mathbb N\dsep
        \NatSeg{r+s}=\NatSeg r\cup
          \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
      &\NatSeg{r+\Succ(s)}=\NatSeg r\cup
        \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}
    \end{aligned}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{r\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \NatSeg{r+s}=\NatSeg r\cup
        \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{r+s\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddClosure}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{r+\Succ(s)=\Succ(r+s)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddSuccRight}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \NatSeg{\Succ(r+s)}=\NatSeg{r+s}\cup\{r+s\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSucc}[thm]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \NatSeg{\Succ(s)}=\NatSeg s\cup\{s\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSucc}[thm]{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall x\,\Bigl(
      x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}
      \leftrightarrow
      x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\lor x=r+s\Bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TranslatedImageSetMembership}[def],
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in A\cup B\eqvdash z\in A\lor z\in B}[thm]{7},
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall x\,\Bigl(
      x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}
      \leftrightarrow
      x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\cup\{r+s\}\Bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in A\cup B\eqvdash z\in A\lor z\in B}[thm]{8},
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}
      =\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\cup\{r+s\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\forall x\,(x\in A\leftrightarrow x\in B)
        \vdash A=B}[ax]{9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \NatSeg{r+\Succ(s)}=\NatSeg{\Succ(r+s)}}{\rIE{5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \NatSeg{\Succ(r+s)}
      =\bigl(\NatSeg r\cup\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\bigr)
        \cup\{r+s\}}{\rIE{3,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \bigl(\NatSeg r\cup\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\bigr)
        \cup\{r+s\}
      =\NatSeg r\cup
        \bigl(\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\cup\{r+s\}\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(A\cup B)\cup C=A\cup(B\cup C)}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \NatSeg r\cup
        \bigl(\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\cup\{r+s\}\bigr)
      =\NatSeg r\cup\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \NatSeg{r+\Succ(s)}=\NatSeg r\cup
        \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}}{%
      \rChain{11,12,13,14}}
  \closeproofpartwideind

  \proofpartwide{Induktionsschluss}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{r\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \NatSeg{r+s}=\NatSeg r\cup
        \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoInductionRule}[ax]{IA,IS,1,2}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung und Koordinaten der Konkatenation]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
      \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon
      \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\to A,\\[-2pt]
    &u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
      i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\vdash
      \WordConcat{u}{v}(i)=u(i),\\[-2pt]
    &u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
      j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\vdash
      \WordConcat{u}{v}(\WordLength{u}+j)=v(j)
  \end{aligned}%
}{WordConcatenationTyping}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v\dsep i\dsep j}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Typisierung]{%
    u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon
      \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\to A
  }{%
    u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon
      \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\to A
  }[WordConcatenationTypingPart]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{u\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{v\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{u}\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{\WordLength{v}\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      u\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      v\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}
      =\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\cup
        \{\WordLength{u}+j\mid
          j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordIndexBlockDecomposition}[thm]{3,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
      \bigl(\WordLength{u}+j\notin
        \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSubsetN}[thm]{3,4},
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddCommutative}[thm],
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoTailAvoidsInitialSegment}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall j,k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
      \bigl(\WordLength{u}+j=\WordLength{u}+k
        \rightarrow j=k\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSubsetN}[thm]{3,4},
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddLeftCancellation}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \forall p\,\Bigl(p\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\leftrightarrow{}&
        p\in u\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength v}\,
        p=(\WordLength u+j,v(j))\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationDef}[def]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}\subseteq
        \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\times A}{%
      \ProofRefStack{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{Funktion}[def]\ (5,6)\\
        \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A
          \vdash F(x)\in B}[thm]\ (6)\\
        \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash
          a\in A\land b\in B}[thm]\ (7,10)\\
        \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\coloneq
          \forall x\,(x\in A\rightarrow x\in B)}[def]\ (5,6,7,10)}}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{%
      q\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,12]{%
      q\in\NatSeg{\WordLength u}\lor
      \exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength v}\,
        q=\WordLength u+j}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordIndexBlockDecomposition}[thm]{3,4,7,12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,12]{%
      \exists a\in A\,\bigl((q,a)\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        (x,F(x))\in F}[thm]{5,6,10,13}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,12]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \forall a,b\in A\,\Bigl(&
        (q,a)\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\land
        (q,b)\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\\[-2pt]
      &\rightarrow a=b\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{Funktion}[def]{5,6,8,9,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,12]{%
      \existsleone a\,\bigl(a\in A\land
        (q,a)\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\existsleone x\,P(x)\eqvdash
        \forall y\forall z((P(y)\land P(z))\rightarrow y=z)}[thm]{15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,12]{%
      \exists!a\in A\,\bigl((q,a)\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\exists x\,P(x),\; \existsleone x\,P(x)
        \vdash \exists! x\,P(x)}[thm]{14,16}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall q\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\,
      \exists!a\in A\,\bigl((q,a)\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\bigr)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{12,17}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon
        \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{UniqueValuedGraphFunction}[thm]{11,18}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Anfangsblock}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon
        \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTypingPart}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{(i,u(i))\in\WordConcat{u}{v}}{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\WordConcat{u}{v}(i)=u(i)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep (x,y)\in F

  \closeproofpartwide

  \proofpartwide{Verschobener Block}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      (\WordLength{u}+j,v(j))
      \in\{(\WordLength{u}+k,v(k))\mid
        k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\}}{%
      \rEI{2,5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      (\WordLength{u}+j,v(j))\in\WordConcat{u}{v}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationDef}[def]{6},
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in B\vdash z\in A\cup B}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}(\WordLength{u}+j)=v(j)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep (x,y)\in F
        \vdash F(x)=y}[thm]{3,7}}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Länge einer Konkatenation]{%
  u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
  \WordLength{\WordConcat{u}{v}}
    =\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}%
}{WordConcatenationLength}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Kürzung bei festem Konkatenationsschnitt]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &u,v,u',v'\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
      \WordLength{u}=\WordLength{u'}\dsep
      \WordConcat{u}{v}=\WordConcat{u'}{v'}\\[-2pt]
    &\vdash u=u'\dsep v=v'
  \end{aligned}%
}{WordConcatenationFixedCutCancellation}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v\dsep u'\dsep v'\dsep i\dsep j}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v,u',v'\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{\WordLength{u}=\WordLength{u'}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{v}=\WordConcat{u'}{v'}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,3]{%
    \WordLength u+\WordLength v
      =\WordLength{u'}+\WordLength{v'}}{%
    \rIE{3,\FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationLength}[thm]{1}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
    \WordLength u+\WordLength v
      =\WordLength u+\WordLength{v'}}{\rIE{2,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordLength u,\WordLength v,\WordLength{v'}\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
    \WordLength v=\WordLength{v'}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddLeftCancellation}[thm]{6,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
    u,u'\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength u}\to A}{%
    \rIE{2,\FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9]{i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength u}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,9]{\WordConcat{u}{v}(i)=u(i)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{1,9}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,9]{\WordConcat{u'}{v'}(i)=u'(i)}{%
    \rIE{2,\FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{1,9}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,9]{u(i)=u'(i)}{\rIE{3,10,11}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
    \forall i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength u}\,u(i)=u'(i)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{9,12}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{u=u'}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{8,13}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
    v,v'\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength v}\to A}{%
    \rIE{7,\FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[16]{j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength v}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,16]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{v}(\WordLength u+j)=v(j)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{1,16}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,7,16]{%
    \WordConcat{u'}{v'}(\WordLength u+j)=v'(j)}{%
    \rIE{2,7,\FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{1,16}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,7,16]{v(j)=v'(j)}{\rIE{3,17,18}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,7]{%
    \forall j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength v}\,v(j)=v'(j)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{16,19}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{v=v'}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{15,20}}
\end{tabproofwide}

      &k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\lor
      \bigl(\exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
        k=\WordLength{u}+j\bigr)\lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\bigl(\exists h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
        k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rOIa{6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[8]{%
      \exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
      k=\WordLength{u}+j}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[8]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\lor
      \bigl(\exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
        k=\WordLength{u}+j\bigr)\lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\bigl(\exists h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
        k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rOIb{\rOIa{8}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,4]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\lor
      \bigl(\exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
        k=\WordLength{u}+j\bigr)\lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\bigl(\exists h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
        k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rOE{5,6,7,8,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[11]{%
      \exists h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
      k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[11]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\lor
      \bigl(\exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
        k=\WordLength{u}+j\bigr)\lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\bigl(\exists h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
        k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rOIb{\rOIb{11}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\lor
      \bigl(\exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
        k=\WordLength{u}+j\bigr)\lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\bigl(\exists h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
        k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rOE{3,4,10,11,12}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Punktweise Gleichheit}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v,w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w},
      \WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}\colon
      \NatSeg{(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+\WordLength{w}}
      \to A}{\FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationAssociativityTyping}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v},\WordConcat{v}{w}\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddClosure}[thm]{%
          \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1},
          \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1}},
        \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{1}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{\WordConcat{u}{v}}=\WordLength u+\WordLength v
      \land
      \WordLength{\WordConcat{v}{w}}=\WordLength v+\WordLength w}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationLength}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{%
      k\in\NatSeg{(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+\WordLength{w}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,5]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\bigl(\exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
        k=\WordLength{u}+j\bigr)\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\bigl(\exists h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
        k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationThreeBlockDecomposition}[thm]{1,5}}

    \proofstepwidestar[7]{k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{%
      k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength u+\WordLength v}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordIndexBlockDecomposition}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1},7},
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in A\vdash z\in A\cup B}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{v}(k)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{3,1,4,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{\WordConcat{u}{v}(k)=u(k)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{1,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)=u(k)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{1,3,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep c=b\vdash a=c}[thm]{9,10,11}}

    \proofstepwidestar[13]{%
      \exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
      k=\WordLength{u}+j}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[14]{%
      j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\land
      k=\WordLength{u}+j}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,14]{%
      k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength u+\WordLength v}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordIndexBlockDecomposition}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1},14}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,14]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{v}(k)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{3,1,4,15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,14]{\WordConcat{u}{v}(k)=v(j)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{1,14}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,14]{%
      j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength v+\WordLength w}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordIndexBlockDecomposition}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1},14},
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in A\vdash z\in A\cup B}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,14]{\WordConcat{v}{w}(j)=v(j)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{1,14}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,14]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)=\WordConcat{v}{w}(j)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{1,3,4,14,18}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,14]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{%
      \rChain{16,17,
        \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{19},
        \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{20}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,13]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{\rEE{13,14,21}}

    \proofstepwidestar[23]{%
      \exists h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
      k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[24]{%
      h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\land
      k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,24]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)=w(h)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{3,1,4,24}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,24]{%
      \WordLength v+h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength v+\WordLength w}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordIndexBlockDecomposition}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1},24}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,24]{%
      \WordConcat{v}{w}(\WordLength v+h)=w(h)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{1,24}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,24]{%
      k=\WordLength u+(\WordLength v+h)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddAssociative}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{1},24}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,24]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)
      =\WordConcat{v}{w}(\WordLength v+h)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{1,3,4,26,28}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,24]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{%
      \rChain{25,
        \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{27},
        \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{29}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,23]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{\rEE{23,24,30}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,5]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{%
      \rOE{6,7,12,13,22,23,31}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall k\in
      \NatSeg{(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+\WordLength{w}}\,
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{\rUI{\rRI{5,32}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{2,33}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Abgeschlossenheit nichtleerer Konkatenationen]{%
  u,v\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  \WordConcat{u}{v}\in\NonemptyWords{A}%
}{NonemptyWordConcatenationClosure}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordTyping}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordLength{u},\WordLength{v}\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordTyping}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{u}\neq0}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \exists k\in\mathbb N\,\WordLength{u}=\Succ(k)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNonzeroIsSucc}[ax]{3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[6]{%
    k\in\mathbb N\land\WordLength{u}=\Succ(k)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{%
    \WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}
    =\Succ(k+\WordLength{v})}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddSuccLeft}[thm]{3,6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{k+\WordLength{v}\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddClosure}[thm]{3,6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{%
    \WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}\neq0}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccNonzero}[ax]{7,8}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordConcat{u}{v}\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddClosure}[thm]{3},
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{2}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{%
    \WordLength{\WordConcat{u}{v}}\neq0}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationLength}[thm]{2,9}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{10,11}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rEE{5,6,12}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\section{Endzerlegung, Induktion und Rekursion}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Eindeutige Rechtszerlegung eines Nichtleerwortes]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  \exists!u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,
  \exists!a\in A\,
    \bigl(w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\bigr)%
}{NonemptyWordRightDecomposition}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep u\dsep a}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Existenz]{%
    w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
    \exists u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\exists a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}
  }{%
    w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
    \exists u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\exists a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}
  }[NonemptyWordRightDecompositionExistence]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordTyping}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordTyping}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\neq0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\Pred(\WordLength{w})\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoPredNatural}[thm]{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{w}=\Succ(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoPredNonzeroValue}[thm]{3,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}
      \subseteq\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSubsetSucc}[thm]{5,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \Pred(\WordLength{w})\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegEndpointInSucc}[thm]{5,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}
      \colon\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{C\subseteq A\vdash
        F\restriction_C\colon C\to B}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{2},7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}
      \in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{5,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}
      =\Pred(\WordLength{w})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthFromTyping}[thm]{10,5,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))\in A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        F(x)\in B}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{2},8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}\in\FiniteWords{A}
      \land
      \WordLength{\LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}=1}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{12},
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordTyping}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}
      \colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{10,11,13},
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddOneSucc}[thm]{5,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}
        (\Pred(\WordLength w))
      =w(\Pred(\WordLength w))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{10,11,13},
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{12},
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegOneMembership}[thm],
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddRightZero}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[16]{i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength w}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,16]{%
      i\in\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}\lor
      i=\Pred(\WordLength w)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSuccElim}[thm]{5,6,16}}
    \proofstepwidestar[18]{%
      i\in\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,18]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength w))}}(i)=w(i)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTyping}[thm]{10,13,18},
      \FormulaRefAuto{C\subseteq A\dsep x\in C\vdash
        F\restriction_C(x)=F(x)}[thm]{7,18}}
    \proofstepwidestar[20]{i=\Pred(\WordLength w)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,20]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength w))}}(i)=w(i)}{%
      \rIE{20,15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,16]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength w))}}(i)=w(i)}{%
      \rOE{17,18,19,20,21}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}(i)=w(i)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{16,22}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w=\WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{%
          \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{2},14,23}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \exists u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\exists a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
      \rEI{\rAI{10,\rEI{\rAI{12,24}}}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Eindeutigkeit]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
    &\forall u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\forall v\in\FiniteWords{A}\,
      \forall a\in A\,\forall b\in A\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
    &\bigl(w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land
       w=\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}\bigr)
      \rightarrow(u=v\land a=b)\Bigr)
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
    &\forall u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\forall v\in\FiniteWords{A}\,
      \forall a\in A\,\forall b\in A\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
    &\bigl(w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land
       w=\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}\bigr)
      \rightarrow(u=v\land a=b)\Bigr)
    \end{aligned}
  }[NonemptyWordRightDecompositionJointUniqueness]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{u\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{v\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{b\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{%
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land
      w=\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{\rAEa{6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{w=\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}}{\rAEb{6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,5]{%
      \LetterWord{a},\LetterWord{b}\in\FiniteWords{A}\land
      \WordLength{\LetterWord{a}}
      =\WordLength{\LetterWord{b}}=1}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{4,5},
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordTyping}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,4,5,6]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}
      =\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep a=c\vdash b=c}[thm]{7,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,4,5,6]{%
      \Succ(\WordLength{u})=\Succ(\WordLength{v})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationLength}[thm]{2,3,9,10},
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddOneSucc}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,4,5,6]{%
      \WordLength{u}=\WordLength{v}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccInjective}[ax]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTyping}[thm]{2,3},11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,4,5,6]{u=v}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationFixedCutCancellation}[thm]{%
        2,3,9,12,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,4,5,6]{%
      \LetterWord{a}=\LetterWord{b}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationFixedCutCancellation}[thm]{%
        2,3,9,12,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,4,5,6]{%
      \LetterWord{a}(0)=\LetterWord{b}(0)}{\rIE{14}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,4,5,6]{a=b}{%
      \rIE{\FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{4},
        \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{5},15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,6]{u=v\land a=b}{\rAI{13,16}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\forall v\in\FiniteWords{A}\,
      \forall a\in A\,\forall b\in A\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\bigl(w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land
       w=\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}\bigr)
      \rightarrow(u=v\land a=b)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{2,\rUI{\rRI{3,
        \rUI{\rRI{4,\rUI{\rRI{5,\rRI{6,17}}}}}}}}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Schluss}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \exists u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\exists a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRightDecompositionExistence}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\forall v\in\FiniteWords{A}\,
      \forall a\in A\,\forall b\in A\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\bigl(w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land
       w=\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}\bigr)
      \rightarrow(u=v\land a=b)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRightDecompositionJointUniqueness}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \exists!u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\exists!a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NestedUniqueExistenceFromJointUniqueness}[thm]{2,3}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Induktion über nichtleere Wörter]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\forall a\in A\,P(\LetterWord{a})\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      \bigl(P(u)\rightarrow
        P(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})\bigr)\\[-2pt]
    &\vdash\forall w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,P(w)
  \end{aligned}%
}{NonemptyWordInduction}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep u\dsep w}
  \DeltaRow{Prädikate}{P}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Rekursion über nichtleere Wörter]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &\exists!F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to X\,\Bigl(
      \forall a\in A\,
        F(\LetterWord{a})=f(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\qquad\forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)
    \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{NonemptyWordRecursion}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep X\dsep a\dsep u}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{f\dsep s\dsep F}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Rekursionsdaten]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X
        \vdash G_0\in M\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl((w,y)\in G_0\leftrightarrow
        w\in W\land y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \WordLength w=1\land y=f(w(0))\Bigr)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall G\in M\,\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl(
        (w,y)\in T(G)\leftrightarrow{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad w\in W\land y\in X\land
        \exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,\exists a\in A\,\bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\qquad (u,x)\in G\land
        w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land y=s(x,a)\bigr)\Bigr)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\mathcal E\colon M\to M\dsep
        \forall G\in M\,\mathcal E(G)=T(G)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &H\colon\mathbb N\to M\dsep H(0)=G_0\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall n\in\mathbb N\,
        H(\Succ(n))=\mathcal E(H(n))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall n\in\mathbb N\,\forall w\,\bigl(w\in W_n\leftrightarrow
        w\in W\land\WordLength w=\Succ(n)\bigr)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &D\colon\mathbb N\to\powerset(W)\dsep
        \forall n\in\mathbb N\,D(n)=W_n
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X
        \vdash G_0\in M\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl((w,y)\in G_0\leftrightarrow
        w\in W\land y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \WordLength w=1\land y=f(w(0))\Bigr)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall G\in M\,\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl(
        (w,y)\in T(G)\leftrightarrow{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad w\in W\land y\in X\land
        \exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,\exists a\in A\,\bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\qquad (u,x)\in G\land
        w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land y=s(x,a)\bigr)\Bigr)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\mathcal E\colon M\to M\dsep
        \forall G\in M\,\mathcal E(G)=T(G)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &H\colon\mathbb N\to M\dsep H(0)=G_0\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall n\in\mathbb N\,
        H(\Succ(n))=\mathcal E(H(n))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall n\in\mathbb N\,\forall w\,\bigl(w\in W_n\leftrightarrow
        w\in W\land\WordLength w=\Succ(n)\bigr)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &D\colon\mathbb N\to\powerset(W)\dsep
        \forall n\in\mathbb N\,D(n)=W_n
    \end{aligned}
  }[NonemptyWordRecursionData]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\colon X\times A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar{W\coloneqq\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{M\coloneqq\powerset(W\times X)}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      G_0\coloneqq\{p\in W\times X\mid{}&
        \WordLength{\pi_1(p)}=1\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\pi_2(p)=f(\pi_1(p)(0))\}
      \end{aligned}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl((w,y)\in G_0\leftrightarrow{}&
        w\in W\land y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\WordLength w=1\land y=f(w(0))\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{u\in A\mid P(u)\}\eqvdash
        x\in A\land P(x)}[thm]{5},
      \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash
        a\in A\land b\in B}[thm],
      \FormulaRefAuto{(x,y)\in A\times B\vdash
        \pi_1(x,y)=x}[thm],
      \FormulaRefAuto{(x,y)\in A\times B\vdash
        \pi_2(x,y)=y}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{G_0\subseteq W\times X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\coloneq
        \forall x\,(x\in A\rightarrow x\in B)}[def]{5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{G_0\in M}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{4,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      T(G)\coloneqq\{p\in W\times X\mid{}&
        \exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,\exists a\in A\,\bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &(u,x)\in G\land
        \pi_1(p)=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\pi_2(p)=s(x,a)\bigr)\}
      \end{aligned}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar[10]{G\in M}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[10]{G\subseteq W\times X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{4,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,10]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl((w,y)\in T(G)\leftrightarrow{}&
        w\in W\land y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,\exists a\in A\,\bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &(u,x)\in G\land
        w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land y=s(x,a)\bigr)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{u\in A\mid P(u)\}\eqvdash
        x\in A\land P(x)}[thm]{9},
      \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash
        a\in A\land b\in B}[thm],
      \FormulaRefAuto{(x,y)\in A\times B\vdash
        \pi_1(x,y)=x}[thm],
      \FormulaRefAuto{(x,y)\in A\times B\vdash
        \pi_2(x,y)=y}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,10]{T(G)\subseteq W\times X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\coloneq
        \forall x\,(x\in A\rightarrow x\in B)}[def]{9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,10]{T(G)\in M}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{4,13}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{\forall G\in M\,T(G)\in M}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{10,14}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \forall G\in M\,\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl(
        (w,y)\in T(G)\leftrightarrow{}&
        w\in W\land y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,\exists a\in A\,\bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &(u,x)\in G\land
        w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land y=s(x,a)\bigr)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{10,12}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \exists!E\colon M\to M\,\forall G\in M\,E(G)=T(G)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TypedFunctionDefinitionPrinciple}[thm]{15}}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        \mathcal E\coloneqq{}&
        \iota E\colon M\to M\,{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall G\in M\,E(G)=T(G)
      \end{aligned}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \mathcal E\colon M\to M\land
      \forall G\in M\,\mathcal E(G)=T(G)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{17,18}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \exists!H\colon\mathbb N\to M\,\Bigl({}&
        H(0)=G_0\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall n\in\mathbb N\,
        H(\Succ(n))=\mathcal E(H(n))\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{DedekindRecursionTheorem}[thm]{8,19}}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      H\coloneqq\iota K\colon\mathbb N\to M\,\Bigl({}&
        K(0)=G_0\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall n\in\mathbb N\,
        K(\Succ(n))=\mathcal E(K(n))\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        H\colon\mathbb N\to M\land H(0)=G_0\land{}&\\[-2pt]
        \forall n\in\mathbb N\,
          H(\Succ(n))&=\mathcal E(H(n))
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{20,21}}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      W_n\coloneqq\{w\in W\mid\WordLength w=\Succ(n)\}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall n\in\mathbb N\,\forall w\,
      \bigl(w\in W_n\leftrightarrow
        w\in W\land\WordLength w=\Succ(n)\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{u\in A\mid P(u)\}\eqvdash
        x\in A\land P(x)}[thm]{23}}
    \proofstepwidestar{\forall n\in\mathbb N\,W_n\subseteq W}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\coloneq
        \forall x\,(x\in A\rightarrow x\in B)}[def]{24}}
    \proofstepwidestar{\forall n\in\mathbb N\,W_n\in\powerset(W)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{25}}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &\exists!D\colon\mathbb N\to\powerset(W)\,{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall n\in\mathbb N\,D(n)=W_n
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TypedFunctionDefinitionPrinciple}[thm]{26}}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        D\coloneqq{}&\iota J\colon\mathbb N\to\powerset(W)\,{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall n\in\mathbb N\,J(n)=W_n
      \end{aligned}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &D\colon\mathbb N\to\powerset(W)\land{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall n\in\mathbb N\,D(n)=W_n
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{27,28}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Stufengerechte Rechtszerlegung]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\dsep n\in\mathbb N
      \vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall w\in W_{\Succ(n)}\,
      \exists!u\in W_n\,\exists!a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\dsep n\in\mathbb N
      \vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall w\in W_{\Succ(n)}\,
      \exists!u\in W_n\,\exists!a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}
    \end{aligned}
  }[NonemptyWordRecursionStageDecomposition]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\colon X\times A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{n\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{w\in W_{\Succ(n)}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4]{%
      w\in W\land\WordLength w=\Succ(\Succ(n))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursionData}[thm]{1,2,3,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4]{%
      \exists u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\exists a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRightDecompositionExistence}[thm]{\rAEa{5}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{%
      u\in\FiniteWords{A}\land a\in A\land
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,7]{\WordLength u=\Succ(n)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationLength}[thm]{7},
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{7},
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddOneSucc}[thm],
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccInjective}[ax]{3,\rAEb{5}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,7]{\WordLength u\neq0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccNonzero}[ax]{3,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,7]{u\in W}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{7,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,7]{u\in W_n}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursionData}[thm]{1,2,3,8,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,7]{%
      \exists u\in W_n\,\exists a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
      \rEI{\rAI{11,\rEI{\rAI{\rAEa{\rAEb{7}},\rAEb{\rAEb{7}}}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4]{%
      \exists u\in W_n\,\exists a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{\rEE{6,7,12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall u,v\in W_n\,\forall a,b\in A\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\bigl(w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land
        w=\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}\bigr)
        \rightarrow(u=v\land a=b)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRightDecompositionJointUniqueness}[thm]{%
        \rAEa{5}},
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursionData}[thm]{1,2,3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4]{%
      \exists!u\in W_n\,\exists!a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NestedUniqueExistenceFromJointUniqueness}[thm]{13,14}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \forall w\in W_{\Succ(n)}\,
      \exists!u\in W_n\,\exists!a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{4,15}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Stufeninduktion: Anfang]{%
    f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X
    \vdash H(0)\colon W_0\to X
  }{%
    f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X
    \vdash H(0)\colon W_0\to X
  }[NonemptyWordRecursionStageBase]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\colon X\times A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{H(0)=G_0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursionData}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{G_0\subseteq W_0\times X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursionData}[thm]{1,2},
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoOne}[def],
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash F(x)\in B}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall w\in W_0\,\exists!x\in X\,((w,x)\in G_0)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursionData}[thm]{1,2},
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordTyping}[thm],
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegOneMembership}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{G_0\colon W_0\to X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{UniqueValuedGraphFunction}[thm]{4,5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{H(0)\colon W_0\to X}{\rIE{3,6}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Stufeninduktion: Schritt]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\dsep
      n\in\mathbb N\dsep H(n)\colon W_n\to X\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &H(\Succ(n))\colon W_{\Succ(n)}\to X
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\dsep
      n\in\mathbb N\dsep H(n)\colon W_n\to X\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &H(\Succ(n))\colon W_{\Succ(n)}\to X
    \end{aligned}
  }[NonemptyWordRecursionStageStep]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\colon X\times A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{n\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{H(n)\colon W_n\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{H(n)\in M}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash F(x)\in B}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursionData}[thm]{1,2},3}}

      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash F(x)\in B}[thm]{2,45}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,32,34]{%
      (\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}},s(H(n)(u),a))\in W\times X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursionData}[thm]{1,2,34,36},
      \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash
        a\in A\land b\in B}[thm]{36,46}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,31,32,34]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists v\in W\,\exists x\in X\,\exists b\in A\,\Bigl(
        (v,x)\in H(n)\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}
        =\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad s(H(n)(u),a)=s(x,b)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rEI{\rAI{31,
        \rEI{\rAI{44,
          \rEI{\rAI{32,\rAI{39,\rAI{\rII,\rII}}}}}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,31,32,34]{%
      (\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}},s(H(n)(u),a))\in T(H(n))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursionData}[thm]{1,2,40,47,48}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,31,32,34]{%
      (\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}},s(H(n)(u),a))\in H(\Succ(n))}{%
      \rIE{43,49}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,34]{H(\Succ(n))\colon W_{\Succ(n)}\to X}{%
      \rRE{\rUE{5},\FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccClosure}[thm]{\rAEa{34}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,31,32,34]{%
      H(\Succ(n))(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(H(n)(u),a)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep (x,y)\in F\vdash F(x)=y}[thm]{%
        51,50}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,32,34]{%
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})
      =H(\Succ(n))(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})}{%
      \rRE{\rUE{\rRE{\rUE{21},
        \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccClosure}[thm]{\rAEa{34}}}},36}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,34]{H(n)(u)=F(u)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{37}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,32,34]{s(H(n)(u),a)=s(F(u),a)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep c=d\dsep P(b,d)\vdash P(a,c)}[thm]{%
        54}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,31,32,34]{%
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)}{%
      \rChain{53,52,55}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,31,32]{%
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)}{%
      \rEE{33,34,56}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall u\in W\,\forall a\in A\,
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{31,\rUI{\rRI{32,57}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists F\colon W\to X\,\Bigl(
        \forall a\in A\,F(\LetterWord{a})=f(a)\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall u\in W\,\forall a\in A\,
        F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rEI{\rAI{20,\rAI{30,58}}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Eindeutigkeit]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &F,F'\colon W\to X\dsep
      \forall a\in A\,
        (F(\LetterWord{a})=f(a)\land F'(\LetterWord{a})=f(a))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall u\in W\,\forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\land
      F'(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F'(u),a)\Bigr)
      \vdash F=F'
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &F,F'\colon W\to X\dsep
      \forall a\in A\,
        (F(\LetterWord{a})=f(a)\land F'(\LetterWord{a})=f(a))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall u\in W\,\forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\land
      F'(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F'(u),a)\Bigr)
      \vdash F=F'
    \end{aligned}
  }[NonemptyWordRecursionUniqueness]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{F,F'\colon W\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
      (F(\LetterWord{a})=f(a)\land F'(\LetterWord{a})=f(a))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \forall u\in W\,\forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\land
      F'(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F'(u),a)\Bigr)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar{P(w)\coloneqq F(w)=F'(w)}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{\forall a\in A\,P(\LetterWord{a})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep c=b\vdash a=c}[thm]{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{u\in W}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[8]{P(u)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,6,7,8]{%
      P(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep c=d\dsep P(b,d)\vdash P(a,c)}[thm]{%
        \rRE{\rUE{\rRE{\rUE{3},6}},7},8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,6,7]{%
      P(u)\rightarrow P(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})}{\rRI{8,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3]{%
      \forall u\in W\,\forall a\in A\,
      (P(u)\rightarrow P(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}))}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{6,\rUI{\rRI{7,10}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3]{\forall w\in W\,P(w)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordInduction}[thm]{5,11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{F=F'}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{1,12}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Schluss}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\colon X\times A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \exists F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to X\,\Bigl(
      \forall a\in A\,F(\LetterWord{a})=f(a)\land
      \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\Bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursionExistence}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \existsleone F\,\Bigl(
      F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to X\land
      \forall a\in A\,F(\LetterWord{a})=f(a)\land
      \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\Bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\existsleone x\,P(x)\eqvdash
        \forall y\forall z((P(y)\land P(z))\rightarrow y=z)}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursionUniqueness}[thm]}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \exists!F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to X\,\Bigl(
      \forall a\in A\,F(\LetterWord{a})=f(a)\land
      \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\Bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\exists x\,P(x),\; \existsleone x\,P(x)
        \vdash \exists! x\,P(x)}[thm]{3,4}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\section{Linksfaltung eines Wortes}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Funktionsgraph eines binären Operators]{%
  \mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\colon A\times A\to A,\\[-2pt]
    &\forall x,y\in A\,
      \bigl(\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(x,y)\coloneqq x\star y\bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep p\dsep x\dsep y}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep G\dsep\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Operatorfunktion}{\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{p\in A\times A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{\pi_1(p),\pi_2(p)\in A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{z\in A\times B\vdash\pi_1(z)\in A}[thm]{2},
    \FormulaRefAuto{z\in A\times B\vdash\pi_2(z)\in B}[thm]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\pi_1(p)\star\pi_2(p)\in A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationClosureAxiom}[ax]{1,3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \forall p\in A\times A\,
    \pi_1(p)\star\pi_2(p)\in A}{\rUI{\rRI{2,4}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \exists!G\colon A\times A\to A\,
    \forall p\in A\times A\,
    G(p)=\pi_1(p)\star\pi_2(p)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TypedFunctionDefinitionPrinciple}[thm]{5}}
  \proofstepwidestar{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\coloneqq
    \iota G\colon A\times A\to A\,
    \forall p\in A\times A\,
    G(p)=\pi_1(p)\star\pi_2(p)}{\rII}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\colon A\times A\to A\land
    \forall p\in A\times A\,
    \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(p)
      =\pi_1(p)\star\pi_2(p)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{6,7}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\colon A\times A\to A}{\rAEa{8}}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{x\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[11]{y\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[10,11]{(x,y)\in A\times A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash
      a\in A\land b\in B}[thm]{\rAI{10,11}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,10,11]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(x,y)=x\star y}{%
    \rRE{\rUE{\rAEb{8}},12},
    \FormulaRefAuto{(x,y)\in A\times B\vdash
      \pi_1(x,y)=x}[thm]{12},
    \FormulaRefAuto{(x,y)\in A\times B\vdash
      \pi_2(x,y)=y}[thm]{12}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \forall x,y\in A\,
    \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(x,y)=x\star y}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{10,\rUI{\rRI{11,13}}}}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Existenz und Eindeutigkeit der Linksfaltung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &\exists!F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to A\,\Bigl(
      \forall a\in A\,
        F(\LetterWord{a})=a\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\qquad \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=F(u)\star a
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{LeftWordFoldExistence}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep u}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep F}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar{\Id_A\colon A\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IdA}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\colon A\times A\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      Q(F)\coloneqq{}&F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to A\land
        \forall a\in A\,F(\LetterWord{a})=\Id_A(a)\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
        F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})
        =\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(F(u),a)
    \end{aligned}}{\rII}
  \proofstepwidestar{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      R(F)\coloneqq{}&F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to A\land
        \forall a\in A\,F(\LetterWord{a})=a\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
        F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=F(u)\star a
    \end{aligned}}{\rII}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\exists!F\,Q(F)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursion}[thm]{2,3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \forall a\in A\,\Id_A(a)=a}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\vdash\Id_A(x)=x}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to A\,
        \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(F(u),a)=F(u)\star a
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \ProofRefStack{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash F(x)\in B}[thm]\\
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]\ (1)}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \forall F\,\bigl(Q(F)\leftrightarrow R(F)\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep c=d\dsep P(b,d)\vdash P(a,c)}[thm]{%
      4,5,7,8}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\exists F\,Q(F)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\exists! x\,P(x)\vdash \exists x\,P(x)}[thm]{6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[11]{Q(F)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,11]{R(F)}{\rRE{\rUE{9},11}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,11]{\exists F\,R(F)}{\rEI{12}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\exists F\,R(F)}{\rEE{10,11,13}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\existsleone F\,Q(F)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\exists! x\,P(x)\vdash \existsleone x\,P(x)}[thm]{6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\existsleone F\,R(F)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\existsleone x\,P(x),\;
      \forall x(P(x)\leftrightarrow Q(x))
      \vdash\existsleone x\,Q(x)}[thm]{15,9}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\exists!F\,R(F)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\exists x\,P(x),\; \existsleone x\,P(x)
      \vdash \exists! x\,P(x)}[thm]{14,16}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \exists!F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to A\,\Bigl(
      \forall a\in A\,F(\LetterWord{a})=a\land
      \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=F(u)\star a\Bigr)}{%
    \rIE{5,17}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Linksfaltung eines nichtleeren Wortes]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \Pi_{\star}\coloneqq
      \iota F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      F(\LetterWord{a})=a\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=F(u)\star a
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{LeftWordFoldDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep u}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep\Pi_{\star}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Linksfaltung}{\Pi_{\star}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Kennzeichnung der Linksfaltung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    \mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash{}&
      \Pi_{\star}\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to A\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall a\in A\,
      \Pi_{\star}(\LetterWord{a})=a\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      \Pi_{\star}(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})
        =\Pi_{\star}(u)\star a
  \end{aligned}%
}{LeftWordFoldCharacterization}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep u}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep F\dsep\Pi_{\star}}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \exists!F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to A\,\Bigl(
      \forall a\in A\,F(\LetterWord{a})=a\land
      \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=F(u)\star a\Bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LeftWordFoldExistence}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \Pi_\star\coloneqq
    \iota F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to A\,\Bigl(
      \forall a\in A\,F(\LetterWord{a})=a\land
      \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=F(u)\star a\Bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LeftWordFoldDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \Pi_\star\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to A\land
    \forall a\in A\,\Pi_\star(\LetterWord{a})=a\land
    \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
    \Pi_\star(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})
      =\Pi_\star(u)\star a}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{2,3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung der Linksfaltung]{%
  \mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
  \Pi_{\star}\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to A%
}{LeftWordFoldFunction}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep\Pi_{\star}}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\Pi_\star\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to A}{%
    \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{LeftWordFoldCharacterization}[thm]{1}}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Rekursionsgleichungen der Linksfaltung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep a\in A\vdash
      \WordFold{\star}{\LetterWord{a}}=a,\\[-2pt]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep
      u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\dsep a\in A\vdash
      \WordFold{\star}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
      =\WordFold{\star}{u}\star a
  \end{aligned}%
}{LeftWordFoldEquations}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep u}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep\Pi_{\star}}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwide{Buchstabenwort}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall a\in A\,\Pi_\star(\LetterWord{a})=a}{%
      \rAEa{\rAEb{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{LeftWordFoldCharacterization}[thm]{1}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\WordFold{\star}{\LetterWord{a}}=a}{%
      \rRE{\rUE{3},2}}
  \closeproofpartwide

  \proofpartwide{Rekursionsschritt}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{u\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      \Pi_\star(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})
      =\Pi_\star(u)\star a}{%
      \rAEb{\rAEb{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{LeftWordFoldCharacterization}[thm]{1}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \WordFold{\star}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
      =\WordFold{\star}{u}\star a}{%
      \rRE{\rUE{\rRE{\rUE{4},2}},3}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\chapter{Volle planare Klammerungsbäume}

\section{Eine konkrete Wortcodierung}

Die Baumcodierung verwendet zwei Sorten von Zeichen. Ein Blattzeichen trägt
ein Element von \(A\); ein Knotenzeichen speichert zusätzlich die Länge des
linken Teilbaums. Diese Längenangabe macht die Zerlegung eines Knotencodes
ohne syntaktische Metasprache eindeutig.

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Alphabet der Baumcodes]{%
  \TreeAlphabet{A}\coloneqq
  (\{0\}\times A)\cup(\{1\}\times\mathbb N)%
}{TreeTokenAlphabetDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep\TreeAlphabet{A}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Baumalphabet}{\TreeAlphabet{A}}
}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Blattcode]{%
  a\in A\vdash
  \LeafTree{A}{a}\coloneqq\LetterWord{(0,a)}%
}{TreeLeafCodeDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Blattcode}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Wohldefiniertheit der Knotencodedaten]{%
  S,T\in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}\vdash
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &S,T\in\FiniteWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}\dsep
      \WordLength{S},\WordLength{T}\in\mathbb N\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
    &(1,\WordLength{S})\in\TreeAlphabet{A}\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
    &\LetterWord{(1,\WordLength{S})},
      \WordConcat{S}{T}\in
      \NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
    &\LetterWord{(1,\WordLength{S})},
      \WordConcat{S}{T}\in
      \FiniteWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeNodeCodeTyping}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep S\dsep T}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    S,T\in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    S,T\in\FiniteWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordTyping}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordLength{S},\WordLength{T}\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordTyping}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    (1,\WordLength{S})\in\TreeAlphabet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeTokenAlphabetDef}[def]{3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LetterWord{(1,\WordLength{S})}
      \in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordConcat{S}{T}\in
      \NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationClosure}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LetterWord{(1,\WordLength{S})}
      \in\FiniteWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordTyping}[thm]{5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordConcat{S}{T}\in
      \FiniteWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordTyping}[thm]{6}}
\end{tabproofwide}

Nach \FormulaRefAuto{TreeNodeCodeTyping}[thm] sind die beiden Argumente der
äußeren Wortkonkatenation endliche Wörter. Damit ist die folgende
Definition unter ihren angegebenen Voraussetzungen wohldefiniert.

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Knotencode]{%
  S,T\in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}\vdash
  \NodeTree{A}{S}{T}\coloneqq
  \WordConcat{\LetterWord{(1,\WordLength{S})}}
    {\WordConcat{S}{T}}%
}{TreeNodeCodeDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Knotencode}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Konstruktortrennung und -injektivität]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &a,b\in A\vdash
      \bigl(\LeafTree{A}{a}=\LeafTree{A}{b}\leftrightarrow a=b\bigr),\\[-2pt]
    &a\in A\dsep S,T\in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}\vdash
      \LeafTree{A}{a}\neq\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},\\[-2pt]
    &S,T,S',T'\in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}\dsep
      \NodeTree{A}{S}{T}=\NodeTree{A}{S'}{T'}\\[-2pt]
    &\qquad\vdash S=S'\land T=T'
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeConstructorNoConfusion}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep b\dsep S\dsep T\dsep S'\dsep T'}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}

    a,b\in A\vdash

  }

    \proofstepwidestar[2]{b\in A}{\rA}

    \proofstepwidestar[1]{(0,a)\in\TreeAlphabet{A}}{%

    \proofstepwidestar[2]{(0,b)\in\TreeAlphabet{A}}{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LeafTree{A}{a}(0)=(0,a)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafCodeDef}[def]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{4}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{\LeafTree{A}{b}(0)=(0,b)}{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{(0,a)=(0,b)}{\rIE{3,6,7}}

    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%

    \begin{aligned}[t]
