\section{Baumstufen und Baumträger}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Erzeugungsschritt für Baumcodes]{%
  X\in\powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})\vdash
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    \TreeClosure{A}{X}\coloneqq{}&
      \{\LeafTree{A}{a}\mid a\in A\}\\[-2pt]
      &\cup\{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}\mid S,T\in X\}
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeGenerationStepDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep X\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Erzeugungsschritt}{\mathcal C_A}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Der Erzeugungsschritt ist eine Abbildung]{%
  \mathcal C_A\colon
  \powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})
  \to\powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})%
}{TreeGenerationStepFunction}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep X}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\mathcal C_A}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    X\in\powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    X\subseteq\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{(0,a)\in\TreeAlphabet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeTokenAlphabetDef}[def]{3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafCodeDef}[def]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{4}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall a\in A\,
      \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{3,5}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[7]{S,T\in X}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{%
    S,T\in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{2,7}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{%
    \NodeTree{A}{S}{T}\in
      \NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeNodeCodeDef}[def]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeNodeCodeTyping}[thm]{8},
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationClosure}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \forall S,T\in X\,
      \NodeTree{A}{S}{T}\in
        \NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \rUI{\rUI{\rRI{7,9}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \TreeClosure{A}{X}\subseteq
      \NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeGenerationStepDef}[def]{6,10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \TreeClosure{A}{X}\in
      \powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{11}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall X\in\powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})\,
      \TreeClosure{A}{X}\in
        \powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{1,12}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \mathcal C_A\colon
      \powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})
      \to\powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TypedFunctionDefinitionPrinciple}[thm]{13}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Monotonie des Erzeugungsschritts]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &X,Y\in\powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})\dsep
      X\subseteq Y\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &\TreeClosure{A}{X}\subseteq\TreeClosure{A}{Y}
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeGenerationStepMonotonicity}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep X\dsep Y\dsep R\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    X,Y\in\powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{X\subseteq Y}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{R\in\TreeClosure{A}{X}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists a\in A\,(R=\LeafTree{A}{a})\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\exists S,T\in X\,(R=\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeGenerationStepDef}[def]{1,3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[5]{%
    \exists a\in A\,(R=\LeafTree{A}{a})}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[6]{%
    a\in A\land R=\LeafTree{A}{a}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{R\in\TreeClosure{A}{Y}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeGenerationStepDef}[def]{1,\rAEa{6},\rAEb{6}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[5]{R\in\TreeClosure{A}{Y}}{\rEE{5,6,7}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9]{%
    \exists S,T\in X\,(R=\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{%
    S,T\in X\land R=\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,10]{S,T\in Y}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{%
      2,\rAEa{10}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,10]{R\in\TreeClosure{A}{Y}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeGenerationStepDef}[def]{1,11,\rAEb{10}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,9]{R\in\TreeClosure{A}{Y}}{%
    \rEE{9,10,12}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{R\in\TreeClosure{A}{Y}}{%
    \rOE{4,5,8,9,13}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
    \forall R\,
      (R\in\TreeClosure{A}{X}\rightarrow
        R\in\TreeClosure{A}{Y})}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{3,14}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
    \TreeClosure{A}{X}\subseteq\TreeClosure{A}{Y}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B \coloneq
      \forall x\,(x\in A\rightarrow x\in B)}[def]{15}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Stufen der Klammerungsbäume]{%
  n\in\mathbb N\vdash
  \TreeStage{A}{n}\coloneqq
  \RecFun{\varnothing}{\mathcal C_A}(n)%
}{TreeStageDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep n\dsep\TreeStage{A}{n}}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\mathcal C_A}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Baumstufe}{\TreeStage{A}{n}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Rekursionsgleichungen der Baumstufen]{%
  \TreeStage{A}{0}=\varnothing\dsep
  n\in\mathbb N\vdash
  \TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}=\TreeClosure{A}{\TreeStage{A}{n}}%
}{TreeStageEquations}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep n}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \mathcal C_A\colon
    \powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})
    \to\powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeGenerationStepFunction}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\varnothing\in
    \powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\varnothing\subseteq A}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{0\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoZero}[ax]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \RecFun{\varnothing}{\mathcal C_A}(0)=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{m_0\in M\dsep f\colon M\to M \vdash
      \RecFun{m_0}{f}(0)=m_0}[thm]{2,1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\TreeStage{A}{0}=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStageDef}[def]{3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[6]{n\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[6]{\Succ(n)\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccClosure}[ax]{6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[6]{%
    \RecFun{\varnothing}{\mathcal C_A}(\Succ(n))
      =\mathcal C_A(\RecFun{\varnothing}{\mathcal C_A}(n))}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{m_0\in M\dsep f\colon M\to M\dsep n\in\mathbb{N}\vdash
      \RecFun{m_0}{f}(\Succ(n))=f(\RecFun{m_0}{f}(n))}[thm]{2,1,6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[6]{%
    \TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}=\TreeClosure{A}{\TreeStage{A}{n}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStageDef}[def]{6,7,8}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung der Baumstufen]{%
  n\in\mathbb N\vdash
  \TreeStage{A}{n}\in
    \powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})\dsep
  \TreeStage{A}{n}\subseteq

  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%

      \powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A
      \vdash F(x)\in B}[thm]{4,1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \TreeStage{A}{n}\in

  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Monotonie der Baumstufen]{%
  n\in\mathbb N\vdash
  \TreeStage{A}{n}\subseteq\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}%

  \proofpartwideindR[Induktionsschritt]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &n\in\mathbb N\dsep
        \TreeStage{A}{n}\subseteq\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
      &\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}
        \subseteq\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(\Succ(n))}
    \end{aligned}
  }{%

      &\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}
        \subseteq\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(\Succ(n))}
    \end{aligned}
  }[TreeStageMonotoneStep]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{n\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \TreeStage{A}{n}\subseteq\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\Succ(n)\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccClosure}[ax]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \TreeStage{A}{n},\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Additive Verschachtelung der Baumstufen]{%

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Menge der vollen planaren Klammerungsbäume]{%
  \BracketTreeSet{A}\coloneqq
  \bigl\{R\in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}\bigm|
    \exists n\in\mathbb N\,
      (R\in\TreeStage{A}{n})\bigr\}%
}{FullPlanarBinaryTreeSetDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep R\dsep n\dsep\BracketTreeSet{A}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Klammerungsbäume}{\BracketTreeSet{A}}
}

  n\in\mathbb N\vdash

}{TreeStageSubsetTreeSet}{%

}

      \NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStageTyping}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
    R\in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{3,2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
    \exists k\in\mathbb N\,(R\in\TreeStage{A}{k})}{%
    \rEI{\rAI{1,2}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{R\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FullPlanarBinaryTreeSetDef}[def]{4,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \forall R\,
      (R\in\TreeStage{A}{n}\rightarrow R\in\BracketTreeSet{A})}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{2,6}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \TreeStage{A}{n}\subseteq\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B \coloneq
      \forall x\,(x\in A\rightarrow x\in B)}[def]{7}}
\end{tabproofwide}

    \proofstepwidestar[8]{R\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rEE{8,9,12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{R\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
      \rOE{1,2,7,8,13}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Schluss}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        R\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\ \leftrightarrow{}&
          \exists a\in A\,(R=\LeafTree{A}{a})\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
        &\exists S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
          (R=\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rLRI{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeConstructorDecompositionForward}[thm],
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeConstructorDecompositionBackward}[thm]}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\section{Strukturinduktion und Strukturrekursion}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Strukturinduktion auf Klammerungsbäumen]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\forall a\in A\,P(\LeafTree{A}{a})\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      \bigl(P(S)\land P(T)\rightarrow
        P(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\bigr)\\[-2pt]
    &\vdash\forall R\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,P(R)
  \end{aligned}%
}{FullPlanarBinaryTreeStructuralInduction}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep R\dsep S\dsep T\dsep n}
  \DeltaRow{Prädikate}{P}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Induktionsanfang]{%
    \forall R\in\TreeStage{A}{0}\,P(R)
  }{%
    \forall R\in\TreeStage{A}{0}\,P(R)
  }[TreeStructuralInductionBase]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{R\in\TreeStage{A}{0}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{\TreeStage{A}{0}=\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStageEquations}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{R\in\varnothing}{\rIE{2,1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{R\notin\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x \not\in \varnothing}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\bot}{\rBI{4,3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{P(R)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\bot\vdash P}[thm]{5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \forall R\in\TreeStage{A}{0}\,P(R)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{1,6}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Induktionsschritt]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &n\in\mathbb N\dsep
        \forall a\in A\,P(\LeafTree{A}{a})\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        \bigl(P(S)\land P(T)\rightarrow
          P(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\bigr)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall R\in\TreeStage{A}{n}\,P(R)
        \vdash
        \forall R\in\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}\,P(R)
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &n\in\mathbb N\dsep
        \forall a\in A\,P(\LeafTree{A}{a})\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        \bigl(P(S)\land P(T)\rightarrow
          P(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\bigr)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall R\in\TreeStage{A}{n}\,P(R)
        \vdash
        \forall R\in\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}\,P(R)
    \end{aligned}
  }[TreeStructuralInductionStep]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{n\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \forall a\in A\,P(\LeafTree{A}{a})}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        \bigl(P(S)\land P(T)\rightarrow
          P(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\bigr)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{%
      \forall R\in\TreeStage{A}{n}\,P(R)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{%
      R\in\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,5]{%
      R\in\TreeClosure{A}{\TreeStage{A}{n}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStageEquations}[thm]{1,5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,5]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &\exists a\in A\,(R=\LeafTree{A}{a})\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
        &\exists S,T\in\TreeStage{A}{n}\,
          (R=\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeGenerationStepDef}[def]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStageTyping}[thm]{1},6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[8]{%
      \exists a\in A\,(R=\LeafTree{A}{a})}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[9]{%
      a\in A\land R=\LeafTree{A}{a}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,9]{P(\LeafTree{A}{a})}{%
      \rRE{\rAEa{9},\rUE{2}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,9]{P(R)}{\rIE{\rAEb{9},10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,8]{P(R)}{\rEE{8,9,11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[13]{%
      \exists S,T\in\TreeStage{A}{n}\,
        (R=\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[14]{%
      S\in\TreeStage{A}{n}\land
      T\in\TreeStage{A}{n}\land
        R=\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[14]{S\in\TreeStage{A}{n}}{\rAEa{14}}
    \proofstepwidestar[14]{T\in\TreeStage{A}{n}}{\rAEa{\rAEb{14}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[14]{R=\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}{\rAEb{\rAEb{14}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,14]{S\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStageSubsetTreeSet}[thm]{1},15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,14]{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStageSubsetTreeSet}[thm]{1},16}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,14]{P(S)\land P(T)}{%
      \rAI{\rRE{15,\rUE{4}},\rRE{16,\rUE{4}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,4,14]{P(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})}{%
      \rRE{20,
        \rRE{19,\rUE{\rRE{18,\rUE{3}}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,4,14]{P(R)}{\rIE{17,21}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,3,4,13]{P(R)}{\rEE{13,14,22}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5]{P(R)}{%
      \rOE{7,8,12,13,23}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4]{%
      \forall R\in\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}\,P(R)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{5,24}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Induktionsschluss}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall a\in A\,P(\LeafTree{A}{a})}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        \bigl(P(S)\land P(T)\rightarrow
          P(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\bigr)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall n\in\mathbb N\,
        \forall R\in\TreeStage{A}{n}\,P(R)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoInductionRule}[ax]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStructuralInductionBase}[thm],
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStructuralInductionStep}[thm]{1,2}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{R\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{%

    \proofstepwidestar[6]{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,6]{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,6]{P(R)}{%
      \rRE{\rAEb{6},\rUE{7}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,4]{P(R)}{\rEE{5,6,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall R\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,P(R)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{4,9}}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Baum-Rekursionsoperator]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X\dsep
      G\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A}\times X)\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)\coloneqq
      \bigl\{(\LeafTree{A}{a},f(a))\bigm|a\in A\bigr\}\\[-2pt]
    &\qquad\cup
      \bigl\{(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},g(x,y))\bigm|
        (S,x)\in G\land(T,y)\in G\bigr\}
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeRecursionOperatorDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep X\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T\dsep x\dsep y\dsep G}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{f\dsep g\dsep\mathcal R_{f,g}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Baum-Rekursionsoperator}{\mathcal R_{f,g}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Transformation eines typisierten Baumgraphen]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X\dsep
      D\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A})\dsep G\colon D\to X
      \vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)\colon\TreeClosure{A}{D}\to X\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall a\in A\,
      \mathcal R_{f,g}(G)(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall S,T\in D\,
      \mathcal R_{f,g}(G)(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})
        =g(G(S),G(T))
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeRecursionOperatorTransform}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{%
    A\dsep X\dsep D\dsep G\dsep Q\dsep p\dsep
    a\dsep b\dsep S\dsep T\dsep S'\dsep T'\dsep x\dsep y\dsep z\dsep w}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{f\dsep g\dsep\mathcal R_{f,g}}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Vorbereitung]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X\dsep
        D\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A})\dsep G\colon D\to X
        \vdash{}\\[-2pt]
      &D\in\powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})\dsep
        G\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A}\times X)
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X\dsep
        D\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A})\dsep G\colon D\to X
        \vdash{}\\[-2pt]
      &D\in\powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})\dsep
        G\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A}\times X)
    \end{aligned}
  }[TreeRecursionOperatorPreparation]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{g\colon X\times X\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{D\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A})}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{G\colon D\to X}{\rA}

      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \BracketTreeSet{A}\subseteq
        \NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%

    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%

    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      D\in\powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})}{%

    \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{%
      G\subseteq\BracketTreeSet{A}\times X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{Funktion}[def]{4,5},
      \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash

      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{%
      G\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A}\times X)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{9}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Trägertypisierung]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X\dsep
        D\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A})\dsep G\colon D\to X
        \vdash{}\\[-2pt]
      &\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)\subseteq\TreeClosure{A}{D}\times X
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X\dsep

        \vdash{}\\[-2pt]
      &\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)\subseteq\TreeClosure{A}{D}\times X
    \end{aligned}
  }[TreeRecursionOperatorCarrier]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{g\colon X\times X\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{D\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A})}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{G\colon D\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{p\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &\exists a\in A\,
          p=(\LeafTree{A}{a},f(a))\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
        &\exists S,T\in D\,\exists x,y\in X\,\bigl(
          (S,x)\in G\land(T,y)\in G\land
          p=(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},g(x,y))\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionOperatorDef}[def]{%

          \rAEa{8}},

    \proofstepwidestar[12]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &S,T\in D\land x,y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
        &(S,x)\in G\land(T,y)\in G\land
          p=(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},g(x,y))

    \proofstepwidestar[3,12]{%

  \proofpartwideindR[Blattfasern]{%

        \vdash{}\\[-2pt]

        \bigl((\LeafTree{A}{a},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)
          \leftrightarrow z=f(a)\bigr)

  }[TreeRecursionOperatorLeafFiber]

    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5]{%

        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionOperatorPreparation}[thm]{1,2,3,4},5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,26]{%
      (\LeafTree{A}{a},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)}{\rIE{26,27}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5]{%
      z=f(a)\rightarrow
        (\LeafTree{A}{a},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)}{%
      \rRI{26,28}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5]{%
      (\LeafTree{A}{a},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)
        \leftrightarrow z=f(a)}{\rLRI{25,29}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5]{%
      \forall z\,\bigl((\LeafTree{A}{a},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)
        \leftrightarrow z=f(a)\bigr)}{\rUI{30}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4]{%
      \forall a\in A\,\forall z\,
        \bigl((\LeafTree{A}{a},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)
          \leftrightarrow z=f(a)\bigr)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{5,31}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Knotenfasern]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X\dsep
        D\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A})\dsep G\colon D\to X
        \vdash{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in D\,\forall z\,
        \bigl((\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)
          \leftrightarrow z=g(G(S),G(T))\bigr)
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X\dsep
        D\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A})\dsep G\colon D\to X
        \vdash{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in D\,\forall z\,
        \bigl((\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)
          \leftrightarrow z=g(G(S),G(T))\bigr)
    \end{aligned}
  }[TreeRecursionOperatorNodeFiber]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{g\colon X\times X\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{D\in\powerset(\BracketTreeSet{A})}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{G\colon D\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{S\in D}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{T\in D}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{%
      (\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,6,7]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &\exists a\in A\,
          (\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)=(\LeafTree{A}{a},f(a))\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
        &\exists S',T'\in D\,\exists x,y\in X\,\bigl(
          (S',x)\in G\land(T',y)\in G\land{}\\[-2pt]
        &\quad
          (\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)
            =(\NodeTree{A}{S'}{T'},g(x,y))\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionOperatorDef}[def]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionOperatorPreparation}[thm]{1,2,3,4},7},
      \FormulaRefAuto{Funktion}[def]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[9]{%
      \exists a\in A\,
        (\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)=(\LeafTree{A}{a},f(a))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[10]{%
      a\in A\land
        (\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)=(\LeafTree{A}{a},f(a))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[10]{%
      \NodeTree{A}{S}{T}=\LeafTree{A}{a}}{%
      \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)=(c,d)\eqvdash
        a=c\land b=d}[thm]{\rAEb{10}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[10]{%
      \LeafTree{A}{a}=\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      D\subseteq\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionOperatorPreparation}[thm]{1,2,3,4}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,5,6]{%
      S,T\in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{%
        13,\rAI{5,6}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,5,6,10]{%
      \LeafTree{A}{a}\neq\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeConstructorNoConfusion}[thm]{\rAEa{10},14}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,5,6,10]{\bot}{\rBI{15,12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,6,10]{%
      z=g(G(S),G(T))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\bot\vdash P}[thm]{16}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,6,9]{%
      z=g(G(S),G(T))}{\rEE{9,10,17}}
    \proofstepwidestar[19]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists S',T'\in D\,\exists x,y\in X\,\bigl(
        (S',x)\in G\land(T',y)\in G\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad (\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)
        =(\NodeTree{A}{S'}{T'},g(x,y))\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[20]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &S',T'\in D\land x,y\in X\land
          (S',x)\in G\land{}\\[-2pt]
        &(T',y)\in G\land
          (\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)
            =(\NodeTree{A}{S'}{T'},g(x,y))
      \end{aligned}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,20]{%
      S',T'\in\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{%
        13,\rAEa{20}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[20]{%
      (\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)
        =(\NodeTree{A}{S'}{T'},g(x,y))}{%
      \rAEb{\rAEb{\rAEb{\rAEb{20}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[20]{%
      \NodeTree{A}{S}{T}=\NodeTree{A}{S'}{T'}\land z=g(x,y)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)=(c,d)\eqvdash a=c\land b=d}[thm]{%
        22}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,5,6,20]{S=S'\land T=T'}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeConstructorNoConfusion}[thm]{%
        14,21,\rAEa{23}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[20]{(S',x)\in G}{%
      \rAEa{\rAEb{\rAEb{20}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[20]{(T',y)\in G}{%
      \rAEa{\rAEb{\rAEb{\rAEb{20}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,20]{G(S')=x}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep (x,y)\in F
        \vdash F(x)=y}[thm]{4,25}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,20]{G(T')=y}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep (x,y)\in F
        \vdash F(x)=y}[thm]{4,26}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,4,5,6,20]{%
      z=g(G(S),G(T))}{%
      \rIE{\rAEb{23},24,27,28}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,6,19]{%
      z=g(G(S),G(T))}{\rEE{19,20,29}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,6,7]{%
      z=g(G(S),G(T))}{\rOE{8,9,18,19,30}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,6]{%
      (\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)

    \proofstepwidestar[33]{z=g(G(S),G(T))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,5]{(S,G(S))\in G}{%

        (x,F(x))\in F}[thm]{4,5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,6]{(T,G(T))\in G}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        (x,F(x))\in F}[thm]{4,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,6]{%
      (\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},g(G(S),G(T)))
        \in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)}{%

        34,35}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,6,33]{%
      (\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)}{\rIE{33,36}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,6]{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,6]{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5,6]{%
      \forall z\,\bigl((\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)
        \leftrightarrow z=g(G(S),G(T))\bigr)}{\rUI{39}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5]{%
      \forall T\in D\,\forall z\,
        \bigl((\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)
          \leftrightarrow z=g(G(S),G(T))\bigr)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{6,40}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4]{%
      \forall S,T\in D\,\forall z\,
        \bigl((\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},z)\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)
          \leftrightarrow z=g(G(S),G(T))\bigr)}{%

  \closeproofpartwideindR

      \rUI{\rRI{32,42}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4]{%
      \mathcal R_{f,g}(G)\colon\TreeClosure{A}{D}\to X}{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4]{%

      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep (x,y)\in F

  \closeproofpartwide

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Strukturrekursion auf Klammerungsbäumen]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &\exists!E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to X\,\Bigl(%
      \forall a\in A\,
        E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\qquad\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))
    \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{FullPlanarBinaryTreeRecursion}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep X\dsep B\dsep M\dsep G}
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{%
    a\dsep S\dsep T\dsep Q\dsep U\dsep Y\dsep p\dsep
    x\dsep y\dsep m\dsep n\dsep k}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{f\dsep g\dsep D\dsep J\dsep H\dsep E\dsep E_1\dsep E_2}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionsoperatoren}{\mathcal R_{f,g}}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwide{Abkürzungen}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{B\coloneqq\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\text{Abkürzung}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{M\coloneqq\powerset(B\times X)}{\text{Abkürzung}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      D\coloneqq\iota J\colon\mathbb N\to\powerset(B)\,
        \forall n\in\mathbb N\,J(n)=\TreeStage{A}{n}}{\text{Abkürzung}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      H\coloneqq\RecFun{\varnothing}{\mathcal R_{f,g}}}{\text{Abkürzung}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{E\coloneqq\bigcup H[\mathbb N]}{\text{Abkürzung}}
  \closeproofpartwide

  \proofpartwideindR[Typisierung des Stufenoperators]{%
    f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X
      \vdash\mathcal R_{f,g}\colon M\to M\dsep\varnothing\in M
  }{%
    f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X
      \vdash\mathcal R_{f,g}\colon M\to M\dsep\varnothing\in M
  }[TreeRecursionStageOperatorTyping]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{g\colon X\times X\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{G\in M}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{G\subseteq B\times X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{p\in\mathcal R_{f,g}(G)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,5]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &\exists a\in A\,
          p=(\LeafTree{A}{a},f(a))\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
        &\exists S,T\in B\,\exists x,y\in X\,\bigl(
          (S,x)\in G\land(T,y)\in G\land
          p=(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},g(x,y))\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionOperatorDef}[def]{1,2,3,5},
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A
        \vdash x\in B}[thm]{4},
      \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash
        a\in A\land b\in B}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{%
      \exists a\in A\,p=(\LeafTree{A}{a},f(a))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[8]{%
      a\in A\land p=(\LeafTree{A}{a},f(a))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,8]{p\in B\times X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\dsep b\in B
        \vdash(a,b)\in A\times B}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{TreeConstructorClosure}[thm]{\rAEa{8}},
        \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A
          \vdash F(x)\in B}[thm]{1,\rAEa{8}}},
      \rAEb{8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{p\in B\times X}{\rEE{7,8,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[11]{%
      \exists S,T\in B\,\exists x,y\in X\,\bigl(
        (S,x)\in G\land(T,y)\in G\land
        p=(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},g(x,y))\bigr)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &S,T\in B\land x,y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
        &(S,x)\in G\land(T,y)\in G\land
          p=(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T},g(x,y))
      \end{aligned}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}\in B}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeConstructorClosure}[thm]{\rAEa{12}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{(x,y)\in X\times X}{%

      \NodeTree{A}{S}{T}\in\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStageEquations}[thm]{20,27}}
    \proofstepwidestar[19]{\Succ(n)\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccClosure}[ax]{20}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,19]{%
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=H(\Succ(n))(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionFunction}[thm]{1,2,29,28}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,19]{%
      E(S)=H(n)(S)\land E(T)=H(n)(T)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionFunction}[thm]{1,2,20,21}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,16,17,19]{%
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))}{%
      \rIE{30,26,25,31}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,16,17]{%
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))}{\rEE{18,19,32}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{16,\rUI{\rRI{17,33}}}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Eindeutigkeit]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &E_1,E_2\colon B\to X\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall a\in A\,
        E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep
        \forall a\in A\,
        E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in B\,
        E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in B\,
        E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))
        \vdash E_1=E_2
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &E_1,E_2\colon B\to X\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall a\in A\,
        E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep
        \forall a\in A\,
        E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in B\,
        E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in B\,
        E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))
        \vdash E_1=E_2
    \end{aligned}
  }[TreeRecursionUniqueness]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{E_1,E_2\colon B\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{%
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{%
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,6]{E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{%
      \rRE{6,\rUE{2}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,6]{E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{%
      \rRE{6,\rUE{3}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,6]{%
      E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep c=b\vdash a=c}[thm]{7,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{6,9}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[11]{S\in B}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{T\in B}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[13]{%
      E_1(S)=E_2(S)\land E_1(T)=E_2(T)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,11,12]{%
      E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))}{%
      \rRE{12,\rUE{\rRE{11,\rUE{4}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[5,11,12]{%
      E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))}{%
      \rRE{12,\rUE{\rRE{11,\rUE{5}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[13]{%
      g(E_1(S),E_1(T))=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))}{%
      \rIE{\rAEa{13},\rAEb{13}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,5,11,12,13]{%
      E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})}{%
      \rIE{14,16,15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,5,11,12]{%
      E_1(S)=E_2(S)\land E_1(T)=E_2(T)\rightarrow
        E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})}{%
      \rRI{13,17}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,5]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        \forall S,T\in B\,\bigl(&E_1(S)=E_2(S)\land
          E_1(T)=E_2(T)\rightarrow{}\\[-2pt]
        &E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})
          =E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{11,\rUI{\rRI{12,18}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,4,5]{%
      \forall Q\in B\,E_1(Q)=E_2(Q)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FullPlanarBinaryTreeStructuralInduction}[thm]{10,19}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5]{E_1=E_2}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{1,20}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Schluss}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{g\colon X\times X\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{E\colon B\to X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionFunction}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall a\in A\,E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionEquations}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionEquations}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        \exists!E\colon B\to X\,\Bigl(&
          \forall a\in A\,E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\land{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall S,T\in B\,
          E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \UEI{3,4,5,\FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUniqueness}[thm]}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\chapter{Blattwörter, Klammerungen und Auswertung}

\section{Blattwort}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Einbuchstabenabbildung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\operatorname{ein}_A\colon A\to\NonemptyWords{A},\\[-2pt]
    &\forall a\in A\,
      \bigl(\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\coloneqq\LetterWord{a}\bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{LetterWordFunctionDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\operatorname{ein}_A}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Einbuchstabenabbildung}{\operatorname{ein}_A}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall a\in A\,
      \bigl(\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}\bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{1,2}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \exists!F\,\Bigl(
      F\colon A\to\NonemptyWords{A}\land
      \forall a\in A\,F(a)=\LetterWord{a}
    \Bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TypedFunctionDefinitionPrinciple}[thm]{3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Die Konkatenation ist ein binärer Operator auf
nichtleeren Wörtern]{%
  \mathsf{BinOp}(\NonemptyWords{A},\frown)%
}{NonemptyWordConcatenationBinaryOperation}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\frown}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationClosure}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}(\NonemptyWords{A},\frown)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationDef}[def]{2}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Existenz und Eindeutigkeit der Blattwortabbildung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\exists!F\colon
      \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
        \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}(F(S),F(T))
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeLeafWordExistenceUnique}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{F\dsep\operatorname{ein}_A\dsep\frown}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \operatorname{ein}_A\colon A\to\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFunctionDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}(\NonemptyWords{A},\frown)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationBinaryOperation}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}\colon
      \NonemptyWords{A}\times\NonemptyWords{A}
      \to\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists!F\colon
        \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall a\in A\,
        F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
          \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}(F(S),F(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FullPlanarBinaryTreeRecursion}[thm]{1,3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Blattwort eines Klammerungsbaums]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\operatorname{wort}_A\coloneqq
      \iota F\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
        \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}(F(S),F(T))
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeLeafWordDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\operatorname{wort}_A}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Blattwort}{\operatorname{wort}_A}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung und Rekursionsgleichungen des Blattwortes]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\operatorname{wort}_A\colon
      \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A},\\[-2pt]
    &a\in A\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a},\\[-2pt]
    &S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeLeafWordEquations}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\operatorname{wort}_A}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\operatorname{wort}_A\colon
        \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\Bigl(
        \forall a\in A\,
          \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)
        \ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad
        \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
          \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
          =\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}
            (\TreeLeafWord{A}{S},\TreeLeafWord{A}{T})
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordExistenceUnique}[thm]},
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \operatorname{wort}_A\colon
      \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rAEa{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall a\in A\,
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)}{%
    \rAEa{\rAEb{1}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}
        (\TreeLeafWord{A}{S},\TreeLeafWord{A}{T})}{%
    \rAEb{\rAEb{1}}}

  \proofstepwidestar[5]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[5]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)}{%
    \rRE{\rUE{3},5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[5]{\operatorname{ein}_A(a)=\LetterWord{a}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFunctionDef}[def]{5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[5]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep b=c\vdash a=c}[thm]{6,7}}

  \proofstepwidestar[9]{S\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[9]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{S}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
      F(x)\in B}[thm]{2,9}}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{T}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
      F(x)\in B}[thm]{2,10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
    =\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}
      (\TreeLeafWord{A}{S},\TreeLeafWord{A}{T})}{%
    \rRE{10,\rRE{9,\rUE{\rUE{4}}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}
      (\TreeLeafWord{A}{S},\TreeLeafWord{A}{T})
    =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationBinaryOperation}[thm],
      11,12}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
    =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep b=c\vdash a=c}[thm]{13,14}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\section{Klammerungen eines Wortes}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Klammerungsmenge eines Nichtleerwortes]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  \WordBracketings{A}{w}\coloneqq
  \bigl\{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\bigm|
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=w\bigr\}%
}{WordBracketingSetDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Klammerungsmenge}{\WordBracketings{A}{w}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Elementkriterium der Klammerungsmenge]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  T\in\WordBracketings{A}{w}\eqvdash
  T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\dsep\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=w%
}{WordBracketingMembership}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep T}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{w}
      =\{S\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\mid\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}=w\}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordBracketingSetDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    T\in\WordBracketings{A}{w}\eqvdash
    T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\dsep\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=w}{%
    \rIE{2,\FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{u\in A\mid P(u)\}\eqvdash
      x\in A\land P(x)}[thm]}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Existenz einer Klammerung]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  \WordBracketings{A}{w}\neq\varnothing%
}{WordBracketingExistence}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep a\dsep u\dsep T}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeConstructorClosure}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordEquations}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}{%
    \rAI{3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in
      \WordBracketings{A}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordBracketingMembership}[thm]{2,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{\LetterWord{a}}\neq\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\vdash A\neq\varnothing}[thm]{6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall a\in A\,
      \bigl(\WordBracketings{A}{\LetterWord{a}}\neq\varnothing\bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{1,7}}}

  \proofstepwidestar[9]{u\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[11]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{u}\neq\varnothing}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[11]{%
    \exists T\,\bigl(T\in\WordBracketings{A}{u}\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\neq\varnothing\eqvdash
      \exists x\,(x\in A)}[thm]{11}}
  \proofstepwidestar[13]{T\in\WordBracketings{A}{u}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,13]{%
    T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=u}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordBracketingMembership}[thm]{9,13}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,13]{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rAEa{14}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,13]{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=u}{\rAEb{14}}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeConstructorClosure}[thm]{10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeConstructorClosure}[thm]{15,17}}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordEquations}[thm]{10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}
    =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}
      {\TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordEquations}[thm]{15,17}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}
    =\WordConcat{u}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}}{%
    \rIE{16,20}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}
    =\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
    \rIE{19,21}}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFacts}[thm]{10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationClosure}[thm]{9,23}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}
      =\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
    \rAI{18,22}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}
    \in\WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordBracketingMembership}[thm]{24,25}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
      \neq\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\vdash A\neq\varnothing}[thm]{26}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,11]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
      \neq\varnothing}{%
    \rEE{12,13,27}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{u}\neq\varnothing\to
    \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
      \neq\varnothing}{%
    \rRI{11,28}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9]{%
    \forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      \WordBracketings{A}{u}\neq\varnothing\to
      \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
        \neq\varnothing\Bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{10,29}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      \WordBracketings{A}{u}\neq\varnothing\to
      \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
        \neq\varnothing\Bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{9,30}}}

  \proofstepwidestar[32]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[32]{\WordBracketings{A}{w}\neq\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordInduction}[thm]{8,31,32}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\section{Auswertung eines Klammerungsbaums}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Existenz und Eindeutigkeit der Baumauswertung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \exists!E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
        \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(E(S),E(T))
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeEvaluationExistenceUnique}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep E\dsep\Id_A}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\Id_A\colon A\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IdA}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\colon A\times A\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists!E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall a\in A\,
        E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
          \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(E(S),E(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FullPlanarBinaryTreeRecursion}[thm]{2,3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Auswertung eines Klammerungsbaums]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\coloneqq
      \iota E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
        \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(E(S),E(T))
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeEvaluationDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep\operatorname{ev}_{\star}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Baumauswertung}{\operatorname{ev}_{\star}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung und Rekursionsgleichungen der Baumauswertung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A,\\[-2pt]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep a\in A\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=a,\\[-2pt]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep
      S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeEvaluationEquations}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep\operatorname{ev}_{\star}}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\Bigl(
        \forall a\in A\,
          \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\Id_A(a)
        \ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad
        \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
          \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
          =\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}
            (\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S},\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T})
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeEvaluationExistenceUnique}[thm]{1}},
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeEvaluationDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A}{\rAEa{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \forall a\in A\,
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\Id_A(a)}{%
    \rAEa{\rAEb{2}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}
        (\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S},\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T})}{%
    \rAEb{\rAEb{2}}}

  \proofstepwidestar[6]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{%
    \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\Id_A(a)}{%
    \rRE{\rUE{4},6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[6]{\Id_A(a)=a}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\vdash\Id_A(x)=x}[thm]{6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{%
    \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=a}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep b=c\vdash a=c}[thm]{7,8}}

  \proofstepwidestar[10]{S\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[11]{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,10]{\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\in A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
      F(x)\in B}[thm]{3,10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,11]{\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}\in A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
      F(x)\in B}[thm]{3,11}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,10,11]{%
    \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
    =\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}
      (\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S},\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T})}{%
    \rRE{11,\rRE{10,\rUE{\rUE{5}}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,10,11]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}
      (\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S},\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T})
    =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{1,12,13}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,10,11]{%
    \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
    =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep b=c\vdash a=c}[thm]{14,15}}
\end{tabproofwide}
